OSW — Results

Ascension 2003

29 June 2003

Jointly staged: OSW (Easton, 2002) · OSF (Chicago, 2003)

Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
What this is The Recent PPV results page of the Old School Entertainment hub site, as it stood in the Wayback capture: the Ascension pay-per-view of 29 June 2003, from Reunion Arena in Dallas.
Live from Reunion Arena, Dallas Texas June 29, 2003 The wonders of Six Finger Crisco~!!!! The scene comes to life and the picture alive with colors as we open up inside of Six Finger Cisco’s locker room. The Freak stands in the middle of the room, dressed very professionally in a pair of black jean shorts and no shirt. Dangling from the youngster’s neck and hanging very loosely, the Asylum member sports a snazzy black power tie. Cisco looks into the camera and stares back out to the world with a cheap smile. Cisco: Ladies and Gentlemen, thank you for tuning into Ascension tonight! The crowd’s cheers can be heard in the background as the camera continues to focus in on SFC. Cisco: Tonight I plan on showing you all something that is going to change your lives! Something so creative and useful that I have decided to put MY own face on its can, and endorse it wholeheartedly! The enthused wrestler points towards something next to him. Cisco: Introducing.....Six! Finger! CRIIIISCOOOO!!!!!!!!! The camera pans right to show a beautiful blonde bombshell, dressed in a dazzling red dress that shimmers in the light and reveals enough to make the men in the audience bark like dogs. The beauty holds a painted green Crisco can, which features Cisco’s smiling face in the upper left hand corner and large black letters that read “ Six Finger Crisco” circling around it. The woman showcases the can and blows kisses at it as she slowly caresses its roundness up and down. Cisco: She’s a knockout eh folks? One fine looking specimen. I know I’d like to have my with her. Oh, and....um...yeah....the blonde’s not too bad either. Damn that’s a fine looking canister! The camera focuses back on the woman who seems taken aback by Cisco’s comments for a moment but then brushes it off and goes back to showcasing the can. Cisco: Hell, I could sell the cans with nothing in them and make a buck they’re so freaking good looking....but I’m not here to do that. I’m here to show you the product...INSIDE the can. Allow me to demonstrate the diversity of Six Finger Crisco. The Deformed One accepts the canister from the woman and pops the plastic lid off. He scoops up a hefty glob of the white substance with four of his fingers on his left hand and continues to talk. Cisco: Thank you Angela. Now, many of you think that Crisco is used solely for baking grease....or in some cases sexual lube.... We cut to Steven Smith’s office where he nervously pulls on his collar and looks from side to side before going back to Cisco’s locker room. Cisco: But that was the OLD Crisco. This is the new and improved Crisco. Allow me to demonstrate. The Freak turns towards Angela, who still has that bright smile on her face as she glances at him coming at her with the grease. Cisco: Say Angela, it looks like you could use some hair gel.... Angela keeps the smile on her face and nervously laughs as The Main Attraction plops the glob of Crisco on her scalp and massages it in her long blonde hair. Cisco gets a twinkle in his eye as he styles the beauty’s hair straight up, the Crisco doing it’s job. Cisco: You see! Six Finger Crisco can tame any wild hair that you throw at! But that’s not all.... The Freak takes up another glob of Crisco and motions for the camera to look down at Angela’s legs. Cisco: Six Finger Crisco is also a great shaving cream! SFC rubs the substance up and down Angela’s long legs, who still holds strong with that smile and tries to talk through her teeth. Angela: Wat da ell r u doin! Cisco merely winks at the blonde bombshell and produces a razor. He glides the shaver up and down Angela’s legs several times, getting all the spots where he put Crisco. The camera follows him as he gets to his feet. Cisco: Six Finger Crisco takes the pain away from shaving your legs! Right Angela? Angela: aaah....Absolutely! -whispers- My leg is bleeding.... The Freak nudges her with his elbow and nervously makes the save. Cisco: Six Finger Crisco everybody! The Asylum member takes up another glob of Crisco, this time with Angela slowly shuffling away from him as he turns in her direction. Cisco: Now, watch as I show you how good Six Finger Crisco is as an exfoliate! The Deformed One mushes the grease in Angela’s face as the smile disappears from her visage and she looks disgusted. Cisco smears the globby substance all over the beauty’s face, leaving her covered in grease when it’s all said and done. Angela: You idiot! Get this crap off of my face!!!! She storms off and out of the locker room, waving her arms around in disgust. Cisco: You hear that folks? She said it’s Crisco-riffic! The Main Attraction hoists up the can in both hands for the camera to see as Azala Zameer bursts into the scene with his own can of Six Finger Crisco. Zameer pops the lid off and palms a massive glob of Six Finger Crisco in his huge hand and shoves the entire helping into his mouth, making his cheeks puff out and some to drizzle out of his mouth. Zameer: Azala love it’s taste! Cisco: Six Finger Crisco, in stores now!!!! The fade to black leaving with the image of Zameer and Cisco both holding there respective cans of Crisco and smiling from ear to ear. Scene fades up with the OSE logo in it’s sleek, steel colored letters in front of a half orange, half red sheet of steel, rusted and worn as we are now accustomed to. Slowly, the logo fades away and we are left in black. Violins accompanied by an orchestra begin to play a slow serenade of “Climb Every Mountain,” from The Sound of Music, begins to slowly play. Scenes from Ascension 2002 begin to appear as a wide angle of Mount St. Hell’s End flies into view, littered with various OSW combatants from last year. The scenes play in slow motion, in time with the slow, operatic song as a beautiful soprano voice begins to sing… Climb ev'ry mountain Propaganda double clotheslines Da Westsidaz from behind while standing on tier one… Search high and low Scene shifts to one of Howard Porter, on tier two, running away from Kid Wonder who is in hot pursuit… Follow ev'ry by-way Jim Axtell, with a sledgehammer high over his head, slams it into the chest of Kory Storm. Every path you know Scene continues as Storm rushes Axtell, who drops and hits a textbook drop toe hold. Kory flies off of the first tier and sails through the air, almost in time with the powerful background music, and slams into the unforgiving concrete floor. The music continues as the scenes of carnage contradict the beautiful song. Climb ev'ry mountain Scene shifts to later in the match, on the second tier as Ivan Stanislav Red Scares Mexi, then El Drago, in slow motion and begins to climb to the top tier of Mount St. Hell’s End. Ford ev'ry stream The team of Hessian and Vampir Nosferatu lift the team of Civil Disobedience up in powerbombs and chuck them off the second tier. Hawke and Masters roll painfully down the side of the steel grated mountain and finally stop on the first tier. Follow ev'ry rainbow Immediately following the powerbombs, Dave Gibson emerges in his infamous debut behind Vampir and hits him with a chair which knocks him off the second tier as well. His voice echoes in the background of the song. “SHUT UP AND WRESTLE!!” Till you find your dream Rhett Locke lifts up Graphic Violence in a fireman’s carry and digs down deep, throwing the much larger man off the first tier to the concrete. Graphic Violence hurtles down the dangerous side of Mt. St. Hell’s End, cutting and bleeding more and more as he falls. Scene shifts to the top tier, where Propaganda, Howard Porter, Ivan Stanislav, Sebastian Toomes, El Drago, and Doug Troy all size up one another. The interlude begins in the song. A dream that will need Closeup of Propaganda… then Porter.. All the love you can give …Ivan Stanislav… Toomes… Everyday of your life …Drago… and finally, Doug Troy. For as long as you live Instantly, DT starts to kick Ivan. Porter Russian Legsweeps Wonder. Toomes taunts Propaganda. El Drago merely bides his time. Climb ev'ry mountain Jesus St. Crux and Frankie Bones square off. Crux leaps and knee lifts Bones square in the jaw, knocking him over. Ford ev'ry stream Propaganda slowly and painful back body drops Ivan Stanislav, nearly sending him off the top tier. Follow ev'ry rainbo Reno Davis and Corg fly off the second tier as Mt. St. Hell’s End becomes less busy and more and more combatants are seen being carried away in stretches and paramedics running busily below the spire. Till you find your dream Three men remain on the top of Mt. St. Hell’s End: Ivan Stanislav, El Drago, and Sebastian Toomes. A dream that will need Toomes is pushed backwards by Ivan Stanislav and the hand of Jesus St. Crux, who is climbing up to the third tier, pulls Toomes off. Crux sacrifices himself and both men fall to the second tier. Leaving Ivan and Drago. All the love you can give Stanislav catches El Drago in a cross body block, his back to the edge of the mountain. Above the two men, ’15 Seconds’ blinks. Stanislav sends Drago sailing up and over his head, behind him, to his doom. Everyday of your life Ivan laughs as flashbulbs go off around him, creating a strange effect in slow motion. Until a hand from the air grips his bright red suspender. Nate Dumas’ voice echoes. “IT’S EL DRAGO!!” Ivan lets out a huge, pantomimed roar and staggers backwards. The clock counts down slowly… 3…. Ivan has both boots on the tier. 2…. One boot… 1…. ….Ivan’s military boots are gone from the mountain and he and Drago sail from the top tier to the second. For as long as you live!!! With the third tier devoid of movement, a lone hand reaches up from the opposite side of where Drago and Stanislav was and grips the bloody, rusted mesh of the top tier floor. Rhett Locke. The scene slowly fades out as the song comes to a close and the jam packed Reunion Center comes into view and Mt. St. Hell’s End stands tall and defiant while a smaller OSW ring and OSF cage sit on either side of the huge monstrosity. The crowd goes wild as flashbulbs explode all around and pyro fires from the entryway and the rafters. Nate: HELLO LADIES AND GENTLEMEN AND WELCOME TO OSE ASCENSION!!! Robbie: Damn! Do you have to start screaming already?? Duke: Shaddup Hoss! It’s a pleasure to be here sitting beside an intelligent man like Nate Dumas. Paulo: Yea? Well it’s nice ta sit beside… uh.. Decker too! Nate: In any event, we are here for OSE Ascension and what a wild night it is going to be! Ever single OSE title is on the line! Not to mention the Old School Title which will be decided by the winner of the Ascension Main Event! Duke: That’s true! I remember watching this show my monitor last year Hoss while I was in the PCW. I gotta tell ya, I was impressed by how much athleticism was shown on that mountain. I hope the EMT’s are ready! Nate: I don’t know where to start! The Main Event, Boda vs. Timo vs. Gibson for the World Title! Zameer vs. Politically Incorrect for the United States title! Ivan Stanislav vs. Trashcan Man for the Presidency and the top tier spot! That’s just scraping the surface! Paulo: Wait a damn minute! Don’t youse forget about that broad Tempest and Inphino Blitz for Old School Fighting Championship. I hate dem both, but it will be one helluva fight! Robbie: I just wanna see more raping! Like last year! That was great!! Seeing Aimz get violated like that! All three announcers are silent. Robbie: ….what did I say? Nate: It’s a pleasure to be here with you folks from around the world! We have so much going on tonight, it’s not even funny! No, Paulo, don’t fake laugh! Paulo: Wha? Nate: So lets get this show on the road! Ladies and Gentlemen, welcome to Old School! Aged for your pleasure!!! The Coin Toss As the scene cuts to the backstage area we see Tony Travoli standing by himself near the backdoor. He sits there with flipping a coin up into the air and catching it. As he continues to kill time the camera pans back and we see that it is time for the coin toss that will decide the fate for these two factions. From the left dressed in black leather coats come the DaVatta clan, leading them is Anthony DaVatta, with Fatico looming behind him and Frank Scarpo off to their right. Oddly enough Jinx and Doll are not amongst them. To the right comes the other group, Scott Taylor and Teresa Tomas walk hand in hand as the other three woman Cassandra Chance, Sombra Mistica and Torrance Smith linger back a little bit. The parties gather around the Tony who addresses the bunch. Tony: As you all know this coin toss will allow the winners of the toss to choose the type of match that will follow. As you can see this coin has both a heads... Tony holds the coin out for the group and the camera as well. Tony: ... And a tails. He flips the coin over revealing the tails side of the coin. Tony: Seeing that this challenge was issued by the Wrestlers, the Fighters will get to call the toss. The call will be made by Mr. DaVatta and will be called once the coin is in the air. Tony stands with a leg out almost looking like a runner ready to take off from the blocks Tony readies himself for the toss. Tony: Here we go. Tony flips the coin and Anthony immediately calls it... Anthony: Heads! ...But the coin isn't flipped gently as it sails up and up and back and lands on top of a large stack of metal chairs and the pinging of the coin bouncing it's way deep into the middle of the pile is heard as all members have turned and nod along with each ping until there is a short moment of silence. Anthony: DAMNIT TONY! Anthony smacks the beligered referee in the back of the head as he does so. Scott: So now what do we do? Anthony reaches into his pocket pulling out a quarter and handing it to Tony. Anthony: That's my last coin Tony so do it right dis time. Tony readies himself again but before he can flip it Scott grabs the coin from his hand and looks at Anthony. Scott: I'll flip it. Anthony nods as Scott flips the coin only a few feet into the air and Anthony once again calls it. Anthony: Heads! The coin reaches it's pinnicle and descends downward as everyone hunches over and looks on. There is an errie silence as the coin tings off the concrete floor and rebounds up slightly before landing on it's side and the coin starts to wobble around before falling flat. The camera zooms in closely to show that the coin landed on heads. Scott: DAMNIT! Everyone backs up and Tony takes control once again. Tony: The Fighters called Heads and the coin landed on Heads, Fighters win the toss... What is your choice? Anthony grins as leans in and tells Tony his decision. Tony: The Fighters have decided that the match will take place under Old School Fighting Rules... Scott looks over at the DaVatta's and then back at Teresa before replying to the match choice. Scott: What does that mean? Anthony: Simply put... It's a normal fight... except in the olden days the women were at home in the kitchen and the men did the fighting for them. Scott: But that means... Anthony: You vs the three of us, leave your women out of dis. Teresa: Yall are afraid of facing us. Scott turns and his eyes express to Teresa to be quiet. Scott: You won the toss fair and square... Old School Fighting Rules it is. The four DaVatta's look on smuggly as Scott leads the dejected females away from the scene. Anthony: This will be a piece of cake. Frankie chimes in with a harsh evil chuckle as the four men leave the scene, just as they go, OSF Referee Hans Galenberg happens to be passing by and we stay with him as the DaVatta's leave the area. Hans starts to whistle softly as he walks along and stops abruptly as he sees the shine of the quarter left behind from the recent events. He looks around to see if anyone is still there and when he sees no one is there he picks it up and smiles as he inspects his new found treasure. Hans: That's odd... this coin has two heads on it. Might be valuable. Galenburg quickly pockets the coin and we fade out to our first break. OSE is brought to you by: PWA - Developing future OSE stars for over two years. OSW: Enforcer & Shocker vs. The Maximators vs. The Solution The Maximators Nate Dumas: Well, Robbie, it’s time for our opening match of the evening! It is of course a Tag Team Three Way Dance. Robbie Decker: I’d assume the Solution should run rings all over the other two teams. Nate Dumas: Only time will tell; Mr. Decker, only time will tell. And just at that moment, “Black Hole Sun,” by Sound Garden echoed its way onto the speakers as the crowd spurts some jeers. Evan Maxim and Aaron Stevenson slowly walked down toward the ring to a modest round of boos from the crowd, Maxim trying to get them to hate him more by flipping them the bird. Earl Henrich: Making their way to the ring, at a combined weight of 386 pounds, they are Aaron Stevenson and Evan Maxim… THE MAXIMATORS! Maxim and Stevenson get into the ring, Stevenson needing a little help to get in. Maxim shakes his head and walks toward a turnbuckle himself, awaiting the arrival of his two tandem opponents. Nate Dumas: Enforcer taking on Maxim should be a good scuffle. Robbie Decker: Why bother? They’ll get owned anyway. Enforcer & Shocker “I’M SIMPLY SHOCKING!” With that note, “Rock Is Dead,” by Marilyn Manson takes over Sound Garden. Enforcer and Shocker make their way to the ring, slapping the hands of the nearby fans. Well, Shocker does. Enforcer has already reached the ring, but stops and waits for his fan-friendly partner. Earl Henrich: Their opponent’s, weighing in at a combined weight of 440 pounds; they are Enforcer and Jason ‘Shocker’ Elliot… ENFORCER AND SHOCKER! The tandem proudly thrust their arms into the air as they climb into the ring, Enforcer keeping his eyes on his estranged brother the whole time. Nate Dumas: Only one more team to arri- Robbie Decker: Which just happens to be the best in the match! The Solution “Rock Is Dead” dies out, but only to be replaced by another Marilyn Manson song. “Disposable Teens,” the old song of the former stable in RUSH, brings Cassius Deamont and Chris Matthews out to a round of jeers from the crowd. Nate Dumas: The fans aren’t too appreciative of these two great workers. Robbie Decker: The fans are also complete and utter idiots. Cassius walks out in front of partner, who takes his time to slowly walk to the ring. The two of them reach the ring as Earl Henrich goes their intro card. Earl Henrich: And their opponents, at a combined weight of 485 pounds; they are Cassius Deamont and Chris Matthews… THE SOLUTION! Matthews and Deamont cockily get into the ring, standing in the other opposite corner to Shocker, Enforcer, Maxim and Stevenson, forming a perfect triangle. All six men wait for the bell to start. Nate Dumas: Here we go! Opening match of the second ever Ascension Pay Per View event! Robbie Decker: Way to shill, Dumas. DING! DING! Almost immediately, all three teams begin to circle each other, picking an opponent they wish to fight with. Three pairings are made as Maxim and Chris Matthews square off, while Deamont and Enforcer square off, leaving Shocker to deal with Aaron Stevenson. Robbie Decker: Er, isn’t this a tag match? Nate Dumas: Probably… who knows what’ll happen. Enforcer charges in at Deamont and the two immediately begin trading rights and lefts, the two brawlers just trying to knock each other out. At first Enforcer had the advantage, getting a few shots in on Cassius, but that all stopped when the Powerful former RUSH champion drove a knee into Enforcer’s stomach. Deamont continued to strike Enforcer in the stomach with his knee, before giving him a shot arm Lariat down onto the canvas, from where he promptly continued his assault. Robbie Decker: Enforcer is getting his ass kicked! Across on the other side of the ring, Enforcer’s tag team partner, Shocker, is doing a similar thing to the untalented and bumbling Aaron Stevenson. Shocker leaps up into the air and slams his back down onto the downed Stevenson with a sudden Senton Splash. Nate Dumas: To absolutely nobody’s surprise, Shocker is dominating over Stevenson. Robbie Decker: Why exactly did we hire Stevenson? He’s a worthless piece of shit. The final pairing left fighting out is Evan Maxim and Chris Matthews, who are both fairly evenly matched. Matthews stops Evan’s attempts at driving him down to the mat with a DDT by swinging his body around to lock on a waist lock, trying to lift up a struggling Evan and dump him over the ropes. Robbie Decker: Do it! Do it! Before Matthews is able to lift Evan up, his Tag partner comes dashing over, trying to make the save. Stevenson reaches Matthews, but the ring smart Matthews lets go of Evan and drops his head, sending Stevenson crashing over the top rope and landing with a thud outside. Nate Dumas: There goes Stevenson. Robbie Decker: I think we’re all glad he’s gone. Nate Dumas: Yeah, this means that Evan is without a partner at the moment. Matthews suddenly snatches the waist lock back onto Maxim, but then swings his body around and launches Maxim toward the centre of the ring, into an awaiting Yakuza Kick by Cassius! Nate Dumas: Down goes Evan! Evan rolls out of the ring while Shocker waits for Matthews and Cassius to turn around. Shocker immediately leaps into the air and catches Matthews with a Spinning Side Kick, knocking him down onto his back. He tries to do the same to Cassius, but Deamont picks him up and throws him over his head with a Belly-To-Belly throw… … but Shocker lands on his feet! Nate Dumas: Ouch! Jason Elliot landed very awkwardly on his left leg there. Robbie Decker: Could it mean a potential injury!? Nate Dumas: Poss- Robbie Decker: YES!! Shocker springs up onto the turnbuckle, twisting his body in mid air and takes an unaware Cassius down with a plancha. … 1 … 2 … A now standing Matthews breaks up the pin with an elbow drop to Shocker’s back. He forces him up by the head and shoulder and tries to lift him up for a vertical suplex but Enforcer comes from behind and into a waistlock. With Cassius struggling to his feet Matthews is unable to escape and is taken over with a German Suplex, which is bridged for the cover. … 1 … 2 … Kickout Nate Dumas: That was close. Robbie Decker: Like hell it was. Shocker turns back to Cassius and pulls him into a standing headscissors, lifting him up slowly and sitting down sharply for a piledriver. After rolling back into the ring under the ropes, Evan Maxim rips Shocker off of the cover attempt over Deamont. The two competitors begin to brawl, until Enforcer joins in and sends Maxim reeling back with big elbow strikes. Nate Dumas: Enforcer is really taking it to Maxim. Robbie Decker: Pfft. Shocker joins Enforcer with the elbows, forcing Maxim into the corner. They then grab hold of the top ropes on each side and begin hitting vicious kicks. Maxim tries to hold his stomach to block the blows, but is just hit with more kicks. Robbie Decker: This is illegal, isn’t it? Nate Dumas: No, this is a double team… and Evan Maxim looks like he is getting what he deserves from it. A recovered Matthews begins to lift Shocker from behind, Deamont quickly taking Enforcer after and taking them over for simultaneous backdrop suplexes. As the team hit the mat Evan Maxim stumbles forward a little and drops down face first. Matthews wastes no time and hooks Shocker’s outside leg. … 1 … 2 … Kickout Deamont looks to Matthews after the kickout and makes the same cover on Enforcer. … 1 … 2 … Kickout Matthews then looks to Maxim, looks back to Deamont, and scrambles across to cover his leg. Robbie Decker: Geez, how many covers are these guys going to make? … 1 … 2 … Kickout Matthews shakes his head, rising to his knee, and forces Maxim up by his head and arm. As Cassius lifts Enforcer across the ring, Matthews hooks under Maxim’s leg and scoops him at the shoulder. He waddles to the ropes and body slams him over them to the ground outside. Nate Dumas: Evan Maxim hit the ground outside hard there. Robbie Decker: Maybe he can bring Stevenson back to life out there. Enforcer hits a sudden elbow to Cassius, freeing himself and fighting back. Matthews tries to assist his partner, but Shocker tackles him down the ground out of nowhere. Enforcer hits a boot to the stomach and latches onto his neck. He struggles to hit the move he wants and resorts to punching any part of Deamont he can. Robbie Decker: Ha ha, having trouble now with the Cassman! Nate Dumas: Cassman? Robbie Decker: Shut up man, it’s cool. Matthews rolls to the side, struggling out of the hold as Shocker tries to counter this and bring him back into the hold. They scramble back up to their feet, locking up with a collar and elbow, and they hit the ropes. Matthews gives a massive pull and manages to throw Shocker back over the ropes, but he falls out with him with the force. Nate Dumas: And now Matthews and Shocker are on the outside, only Deamont and Enforcer are inside the ring! Robbie Decker: And Jobforcer is still having trouble! Nate Dumas: Don’t call him that! Robbie Decker: Why not? Enforcer hits one last forearm to Deamont’s back before trying to lift again, he reaches the vertical point and takes a step back, the crowd gasp as they see Deamont’s body being dropped down passed the ropes and hitting the ground outside after a suplex variation. Nate Dumas: Enforcer just suplexed Deamont right out of the ring! Robbie Decker: That was weird. Enforcer turns back to the ring, flexing his muscles as he roars at the crowd. Hopping up onto the ring, Aaron Stevenson makes a commotion and insulting hand gestures to Enforcer, who charges at him and knocks him back out with a lariat. With his back turned, Evan Maxim springs up onto the ropes and flies across the ring with a plancha… but, seeing him in the corner of his eye, Enforcer just catches him on his shoulder. He reaches up to his shoulder and turns him around slowly, so that his head faces the mat. The fans cheer again as he takes a few quick steps forward and drives him down. Nate Dumas: Running Powerslam! Robbie Decker: Ahhh man! … 1 … 2 … 3! Nate Dumas: The Maximators are out of this match! Robbie Decker: It was that shmuck Stevenson’s fault, Maxim would be winning this thing by now if it wasn’t for him. Shocker, getting the upper hand over Matthews, whips him to the ring and forces him under the ropes. Enforcer wastes no time and pulls him up to his feet, Shocker taking a step back to rest as his tag partner has Chris in a standing headscissors. He lifts him up onto his shoulders for a powerbomb position, the crowd cheering as they see the move that could finish Matthews off only a few seconds away… Cassius Deamont, out of nowhere, hits a diving lariat to Enforcer’s legs and knocks him right off his feet, Matthews crashing down on top of him for an easy cover. … 1 … 2 … Kickout Shocker struggles back into the ring, already in Deamont’s hands as Matthews begins striking his clenched fists into his opponent’s head. Robbie Decker: The Solution reigns supreme, things are how they should be. Nate Dumas: I wouldn’t say that so soon, Shocker and Enforcer are still in this equally. Robbie Decker: Yeah right. Deamont hooks around Shocker’s legs, lifting him up easily onto his shoulder. He begins tottering backwards, Matthews shifting off of Enforcer before Deamont drops back and flapjacks Shocker onto his own tag partner. Robbie Decker: Yes! Nate Dumas: What a strange manoeuvre there by Cassius. Robbie Decker: Don’t spoil it with your talking please. Matthews pulls Shocker off of Enforcer and whips him back out through the ropes, leaving Enforcer alone with The Solution. Nate Dumas: This could spell trouble for Enforcer! Robbie Decker: YES! Cassius and Matthews look at each other and nod. Together they simultaneously kick Enforcer in the stomach, causing the former IOW worker to hunch over. Cassius picks Enforcer up by the waist in an Atomic Drop position, but holds him there. Matthews runs passed him and into the ropes, before leaping into the air and taking down Enforcer with a Diving Lariat, in conjunction with Deamont’s standing Spinebuster. Robbie Decker: It’s over, surely! No more Enforcer & Shocker! Nate Dumas: Since when did this turn into “Loser Leaves” match? Robbie Decker: Since I said so, you douche. Enforcer is flat on his back in the middle of the ring, and his partner is crouched down next to the ring steps, holding onto his ribs. Cassius says something back at Matthews, who nods at him. Matthews climbs through the ring ropes and attends to Shocker. Nate Dumas: Shocker is hurt; he may have broken a rib when he was dumped down onto his partner. Robbie Decker: One down, six to go. Nate Dumas: Six what? Robbie Decker: Ribs. Matthews kicks Shocker in the convenient spot that is his injured rib with his trained feet. Shocker holds onto them in a bit of pain as the camera’s return to the action back in the ring. Cassius picks up Enforcer and puts him into a Powerbomb position, but turns toward the turnbuckle. Cassius picks up his foe and promptly Powerbomb’s straight into the top turnbuckle. Nate Dumas: Dear god!! His neck! Robbie Decker: Ye-.. Before Robbie can finish his sentence, the camera quickly swivels back to Chris Matthews, who had just bounced off of the second rope on the inside of the ring and Moonsaulted all of the way over the top rope and out of the ring, driving his knees into Shocker’s ribs on the way. Robbie Decker: ..ah! Nate Dumas: That had to hurt Shocker. Robbie Decker: Well spotted, you idiot. Inside of the ring, Cassius lays a sloppy pin on the downed Enforcer. …1 …2 …FOOT ON THE ROPE! Nate Dumas: Enforcer just got his foot on the rope, and Deamont isn’t happy. Robbie Decker: You wouldn’t be either. Cassius instructs Matthews to get back inside of the ring. Matthews slides back in, immediately going back to work on the Enforcer. Matthews kicks Enforcer quite hard in the back of the head, making the bigger of the two wince. He continues to drive his kicks into Enforcer’s neck, softening him up for a Verticle Drop Brainbuster or Deamont’s Death Valley Driver. Robbie Decker: Show’s over for Enforcer… doo dah, doo dah… Nate Dumas: Stop being so confident! You never know what can happen. Robbie Decker: Well, usually when your partner is having trouble breathing, you’re stuffed. Matthews slowly picks up Enforcer by the neck in a Headlock, straight in position for his Verticle Drop Brainbuster. After a bit of stalling, he drives him down into the mat with the Brainbuster, Enforcer flopping down at an awkward angle. Robbie Decker: Ignition MAX II! Nate Dumas: Matthews tags to Deamont! In the end, the anger will not cease. Because in the end, hatred never dies. In The End, presented only on pay per view by Lights Out Wrestling. Official Interview from the Home of Rhett Locke! The walls are white; a regular white. The white that you'd see on every set of a present day T.V. sitcom. A dining room table, mahogany, rests in the center of a room that has been tiled to perfection; by men who considered it not only an occupation, but an art form to do such a line of work. The pale light of a chandelier rests above both Shanda Russell and Rhett Locke. Locke, the albino, looks less like PWC's version and much more like the OSE one. He'd colored his hair purple, for no particular reason other than to add color to his life. Abnormally white skin, white hair, and white walls could nearly drive a man mad. Especially one who'd been mad before. His bottom half was hidden by the table, his upper-half was merely a distressed t-shirt with Sesame Street's Cookie Monster on it. 'C is for cookie, it's good enough for me', it read. Shanda Russell: Hello everyone! I'm here at the home of Dmitri Locke, known as Rhett to all of you fans out there. He's kindly agreed to do a sit-down interview in this lovely condo. So, I must ask Rhett, how did you get back into OSE after the way you left? Rhett Locke: Well, to be quite honest, when we came to Jane Reagan about it we fully expected rejection. PWC and Ron Starr just weren't working out, as far as that goes, and we were looking for a place that might give us a bit of creative freedom as well as job security. When we told Jane our full situation, she complied and signed us to a multiyear contract. Shanda Russell: Who's we? Is there someone else? Claire, perhaps? Rhett Locke: ...Speech impediment. You, of course, know of my...errr...medical history, as it were. As for Claire, she won't be coming to OSE... she hates living a life in front of cameras. Shanda Russell: Ah yes, my deepest apology if I've offended you, which I'm almost certain I did not. Rhett Locke: Nope. None taken. Shanda Russell: Speaking of PWC, now that you left before the illthought-out closing, do you feel a bit smarter since you left and came here? Rhett Locke: Yes, but we feel worse about leaving the guys in that company behind... we smelled this coming from miles and miles away, and got the hell out of dodge... they didn't. And it sucks. Shanda Russell: Indeed. So, everyone remembers the way you left the fed-- not only how you left but the turn you made toward the 'darkside', if you will, shortly before. You said you hope to win these fans back... how do you hope on accomplishing this goal? Rhett Locke: By putting on outstanding, quality matches first and foremost. When fans see you perform, and you really get their attention, hopefully...they respect you for it. We also plan on standing up for our beliefs... something we didn't do in the past, but something our plans have certainly centered on now. Shanda Russell: I see... so, what made you leave OSW in the first place? Rhett Locke: Our ego grew to the size of a watermelon, basically. We felt like we deserved more than anyone else, respect. Hell, we felt that even Ivan Stanislav, TCM, or Dragon couldn't live up to our in-ring ability... the seeds of egotism grew rich and stemmed forth a new Rhett, so to speak... a different one. Shanda Russell: Understandable, Rhett. It's certainly happened to many people, not just you. Rhett Locke: Well, yes... we suppose. Shanda Russell: This interview airs on Ascension, and since you were the inaugural winner of the Mt. Saint Hell's End match, pick a winner for me. Rhett Locke: No thanks, too many people with too much talent to pick one. Shanda Russell: Anyone that stands out to you, personally, then? Rhett Locke: Ah, you put us on the spot... Six Finger Cisco. The guy is going into this match a complete underdog, which is what we were when we became the first ever winner. But you never know with a match like this, one mistake will eliminate you. Just one. Shanda Russell: Also, it was made clear you have a special announcement for us tonight. What's the announcement? Rhett Locke: Right, the big announcement. Well, this goes to the winner of the Mt. Saint Hell's End match at the end of the show. We, Rhett Locke, are challenging you RIGHT NOW, whoever you may be, to a japanese death match. The ropes will be replaced with barbedwire, land mines will lay about the ring in an undisclosed amount-- and weapons galore. Also, after twenty five minutes... heh, if there isn't a winner, the ring will explode. You and us, whoever you are.... we'll put on one hell of a show. Shanda Russell: Wow! What an announcement!! Rhett Locke: We figure it's a great way to prove to the fans that we're earnest... we're gonna put this match on FOR the fans. No one else. Shanda Russell: What a landmark way to end an interview, Rhett! Rhett Locke: Aww... you have to leave so soon? Shanda Russell: Indeed, we do. Well everyone, this is Shanda Russell from the condo of Rhett Locke, signing off. The camera shuts off. All of the white goes to black. He grabs Magnum in a half nelson from behind, and starts hammering him with closed fists to the back of the neck! Nate: That... that... Duke: THAT’S THE TOMMY GUN!!!! The fans erupt as the masked man punches Magnum repeatedly, until he sags to his knees. The masked man then reaches up, starts pulling off his disguise and- Nate, Robbie, Paulo and Duke: HOLY SHIT!!!! Nate: IT’S ANGELO DEVILLE! ANGELO DEVILLE IS HERE! ANGELO DEVILLE IS HERE IN OLD SCHOOL ENTERTAINMENT! MY GOD! Robbie: And he’s getting some payback against his bitter rival from PWC!!!! Deville starts to pull Magnum to his feet, when a trap door opens behind the controversial competitor-and Miguel’s head pokes out! Miguel helps Cianan out, and they both rush Deville! His face contorts in shock as he is shoved forward-and grabs onto Magnum Wolf for support! Deville goes sailing over the edge, and takes Magnum with him!!! Earl: ANGELO DEVILLE AND MAGNUM WOLF HAVE BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Cianan and Miguel start to congratulate one another, when Cianan’s face contorts in pain! Hiro has snaked out of the bottom cage, and has nailed Cianan in the kidneys with a knee, followed by a German suplex! He bridges, and Mofo counts. One... Two... Miguel stomps Hiro to break it up. Hiro starts to rise, Miguel pulls him close and drops him with a one-two combo to the jaw! Miguel then helps Cianan to his feet and they start to climb. Paulo: Looks like Miguel and Cianan have formed an alliance! Higher up, Shawn Stewart is trying to fend off the approach of Cisco and Gibson. He does not see Timo, however, who is scaling up behind the fighter. Just below, Jeffrey Wayne is only now recovering his vision, as Inphino drops Teresa with the Dark Sweep, and begins to scale. Asa and DarkStar still are slugging it out. Robbie: And Kriegman and CIA still are standing tall!!! The Evil Supergenius and his contracted agent actually are seated on the top, waiting for someone to try an ascent. Timo, who bypasses the waiting Stewart altogether, heads toward the top-but Kriegman and CIA are waiting. Kriegman takes Bob and swings it at the Samoan Silencer, who ducks and slides down the incline part of the way to avoid being brained. CIA takes a different approach, dropping down onto the incline itself. As Kriegman bends over to see where Timo is, a hand reaches up and snags Kriegman in a Tongan Death Grip! Nate: TIMO’S GOT HIM! TIMO BOLAMBA HAS KRIEGMAN! FINALLY, THE EVIL SUPERGENIUS MIGHT BE REMOVED FROM THE SUMMIT! Timo starts to walk up the incline, still holding Kriegman, who since has dropped Bob. Suddenly, the ferocity in Timo's eyes begins to fade. CIA is revealed behind the Samoan Silencer, applying his patented Nerve Hold! Robbie: YES! CHARLES ISAAC ANDERSON, BABY! WHOOO! Timo starts to fade, and releases Kriegman... when suddenly a newly summitted Cianan walks up and delivers a spin kick to the small of CIA's back! The nerve hold lessens, and Timo drops to hands and knees on the incline. Cianan starts to brawl with CIA, as Timo sits back. The three men are converged on one area of the incline--already dented by the impact of Fatico--when, suddenly-- KER-SNAP! The incline gives way! Fans shriek as Timo, CIA and Cianan ALL drop through the hole, PAST the middle cage and land on TOP of the bottom cage!!!! Fans: HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! The referees in the empty bottom cage all start reaching up for the three competitors, who are lifeless. The impact leaves all three bloody, battered and very much unconscious. Pierre Gaston uses a ladder to climb to the top and check the three men, then makes a signal. Earl: TIMO BOLAMBA, CIANAN AND CIA HAVE BEEN ELIMINATED! Kriegman watches the catastrophe, and cannot repress a grin as he looks down at the broken body of Timo. Surprisingly, the fans' concerned mrumurs turn to wild cheers. Krieggy looks up, confused-- Robbie: HOLY FUCKING SHIT! The fans go APESHIT as Trashcan Man pulls himself to a vertical base behind Kriegman. Robbie: LOOK OUT KRIEGGY!!! Paulo: That Trashy is one tough sonofabitch. Trashy spins Kriegman around and lifts him. Back protesting, Trashy connects on the PYRO DRIVER!!! He covers. One... [crowd counts along] Two... Three!!! Nate: YES! YES YES YES YES YES! Robbie: NOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!! Earl: ALAN KRIEGMAN HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!!! Stewart and Cisco still are slugging it out on the middle tier, as Gibson nails Stewart from behind and Miguel approaches. Below them, Hiro is approaching the continuing brawl between Asa and DarkStar. Wayne is starting an ascent to the middle tier, but runs into Taylor, who has equal intentions. Wayne takes a swing at Taylor, who blocks and decks the Legend with a stiff overhand right, sending him tumbling back halfway down the incline. Taylor, meanwhile, continues to ascend. Duke: We're runnin' out of competitors, guys. Elsewhere on the bottom tier, Ivan and Kenyatta are struggling back to their feet. Ivan takes a swing at Kenyatta, who ducks it and jabs Stanislav in the kidneys. Taking advantage, Kenyatta winds up for a knockout punch--but as his blow goes to Ivan's groin, Stanislav's hand reaches out and grabs Jackson's hand! Ivan starts squeezing on Kenyatta's hand, as Jackson screams out in pain. Gritting his teeth, Ivan grabs Kenyatta by the throat, lifts him--and CHOKESLAMS KENYATTA OFF THE STRUCTURE! Fans; HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! Earl: KENYATTA AHMED JACKSON HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Hiro nails Asa with a hook kick to the back of the head, followed by a whirling spin kick to DarkStar's face. Hiro then begins to scale. Nate: WE'RE APPROACHING THE FINAL MOMENTS OF THIS MATCH! Gibson reaches the top behind Trashcan Man, a malevolent smile on his face. Down below, Cisco is actually getting the better of Shawn Stewart, as Miguel Carranza starts scaling to the top tier. Duke: Trashy better look out!!! Gibson runs up, nails Trashy in the back of the head, picks him up for a side suplex and converts it into a backbreaker! TCM drops, agonized, and Gibson makes the cover, as Kriegman rolls lifelessly down the immediate incline. One... Two... Three! Nate: SHIT! Earl: TRASHCAN MAN HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Gibson looks left, looks right and realizes he's on the top. As Stewart brawls with Cisco below on the middle tier and Taylor climbs, and chaos continues on the very bottom tier, Gibson raises his arms in triumph! Nate: ONLY A FEW MINUTES TO GO AND DAVE GIBSON IS STANDING TALL ON THE TOP!!! Suddenly, Gibson's reverie is broken as a brown-skinned hand grabs him by the arm. Gibson is spun around to see Miguel Carranza facing him. Gibson goes for a punch, Miguel blocks it, grabs hold of Gibbo's arm and pulls him forward into a punch to the temple!!! Gibson's eyes roll up into his head, and he flops to the mat! Duke: SERPENT'S STRIKE!! Riggs bends over Gibson, who was unconscious before he even hit the mat. Earl: DAVE GIBSON HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Taylor reaches the middle tier--where Cisco has Stewart locked in the Six Finger Discount. The well-versed fighter drops to his knees, gasping for breath--until he catches the Deformed One with an uppercut below the belt! Cisco drops into the fetal position, clutching at himself. Stewart starts to stand--as Scott Taylor runs forward and catches him with Off to Never Never Land! Stewart's head snaps back, and he hits the lower incline hard, rolling back to the bottom tier. Taylor turns and begins to ascend when he hears-- Voice: TAYLOOOOOR!!! Taylor looks, and sees the military-style haircut of Ivan Stanislav emerge from the lower incline. Ivan walks up, gritting his teeth, blood trickling from a few open wounds. Robbie: UH OH! Nate: Ivan Stanislav has drawn a bead on Scott Taylor! Showing no fear, Taylor steps forward and starts stomping on Ivan as he tries to ascend. Meanwhile, Riggs and Minion are getting the carcasses of Alen Kriegman, Trashcan Man and Dave Gibson down off the top tier. Kriegman is standing, TCM is being assisted by Riggs, and Gibson is completely lifeless. Duke: Damn, Gibson just got DROPPED by that punch. Paulo: That's why these wrestlers are pansies, Duke. Taylor has Ivan at bay, when he sees Hiro emerging on the top tier. Taylor hustles over and nails Hiro with a running forearm, the momentum of which sends Kitigami tumbling back down the incline. Jeffrey Wayne's head pops up as well, but he drops back down before Taylor can punch or kick him. Unfortunately, that momentary distraction allowed Ivan to reach the middle tier! Nate: LOOK OUT, SCOTT!! Back below, Asa Fountain is jabbing away on DarkStar. He stops, claps his hands and spins--going for a poke to the eye--when a trap door opens under Asa's feet!!! The Disco Daddy DROPS down into the bottom cage! Paulo: HOLY SHIT!!! A groggy DarkStar, now bleeding from a cut under the eye, leans forward toward the hole--where a hand reaches up, grabs him and yanks DarkStar into the hole!! Duke: WHOA! A firm hand grips the bottom tier, and a bald head emerges. Nate: Oh, sonofabitch... Robbie: MATT REGISTER! THE SMART ASS IS ALIVE AND KICKING, BABY!! Register rolls out onto the bottom outer tier as Inphino Blitz and Teresa Tomas begin scaling up the middle tier. There, Scott Taylor is gamely firing away on Ivan with a few punches--when suddenly a massive hand reaches up and grabs Taylor by the throat! Snarling, Ivan hoists Taylor, and chokeslams him onto the upper incline! Still grabbing Taylor's throat, Ivan pulls him back within reach, applies a front facelock and hoists Taylor in a vertical suplex--THEN RELEASES HIM!!! Taylor SAILS through the air--and CRASHES onto the bottom tier!!!!!! Nate, Robbie, Paulo and Duke: HOLY FUCKING SHIT!!! Nate: RED SCARE FROM THE MIDDLE TIER! IVAN WITH A RED SCARE FROM THE MIDDLE TIER ONTO SCOTT TAYLOR! Duke: This is a dang war of attrition. Robbie: Did you just use a three-syllable word??? Duke: Fuck off. Robbie: THAT is more like it. Teresa Tomas sees her friend hit and screams in shock and dismay. She starts scrambling back down the incline, and goes to check on Taylor, cradling his unconscious head and neck. Referee Johnny Jones is the first official to reach Taylor, and immediately starts making a motion. Earl: SCOTT TAYLOR HAS BEEN ELIMINATED! Back on the middle tier, Hiro and Jeffrey Wayne have reached the surface. Hiro heads right for the top, while Inphino pulls himself up onto the middle tier. Miguel is on the top, shadow boxing, waiting for someone to rise. Nate: WE'VE GOT LESS THAN FIVE MINUTES TO GO IN REGULATION! AT THIS POINT, MIGUEL CARRANZA IS POSITION TO WIN THE TITLE! Robbie: He can't win; he didn't roleplay. Duke: SSH! Hiro gets up first, and starts to make the final ascent, when his eyes flash in surprise. Jeffrey Wayne, beneath Hiro, is hoisting him in an Electric Chair! Wayne leans back, sending Hiro head over heels back toward the middle tier! As Inphino reaches the top, Kitigami CRASHES into the Miyabe master, sending both tumbling to the bottom!!! Nate: CATASTROPHIC!!!! Miguel is still pacing back and forth on the top, as Jeffrey Wayne reaches the top. Miguel starts feinting punches at Wayne who backs up and circles, treating the pugilist very warily. Below them, Ivan Stanislav is also climbing to the top. The Legend sees Ivan approaching and promptly backs away. Miguel turns and sees the Russian Bear. Robbie: YOU BOTH BETTER RUN AWAY! IVAN'S HERE TO KICK SOME ASS, BABY! Miguel, however, takes a step forward. Duke: He took on Fatico--NO WAY Miguel backs down from Ivan! Miguel is bouncing from foot fo foot, shaking his fists and lightly punching himself in the jaw. The fans are roaring, in anticipation of the conflict. Crowd: MI-GUEL! MI-GUEL! MI-GUEL! Ivan stands on the top, Miguel approaches--and Wayne DROPKICKS HIM IN THE BACK! Miguel goes sailing past Stanislav, and down the incline onto the middle tier, where Matt Register is starting to climb. Inphino Blitz also is beginning a second ascent. On the top, Ivan starts to approach Wayne, when suddenly his progress is halted. Nate; IT’S CISCO! SIX FINGER CISCO IS CLINGING TO IVAN’S LEG FOR DEAR LIFE! Robbie: IT'S GETTING DOWN TO THE WIRE, GUYS!! Register and Inphino both are on the middle tier now, and Inphino connects on a whirling roundhouse kick to the midsection. Register drops to his knees, then promptly sweeps Inphino’s legs out from under him! The Smart Ass stands, and punches Inphino right between the legs! Fans: Ooooooooh. Register hooks Inphino’s legs, and goes for a catapult. He sends Inphino flying—INTO MIGUEL! Both martial artist and boxer are sent careening over the edge and down the steel incline in a tumble of bloody flesh. On the top, Ivan has Cisco by the head. Snarling, Stanislav grips Cisco and pulls him up, dangling the Freak in the air over the second incline. Nate: Oh god No! Not that! NOT A RED SCARE INTO OPEN AIR! NOT FROM THIS HEIGHT! Jeffrey Wayne is content to sit back and watch, observing Ivan prepare for carnage. Suddenly— Duke: Hey, look! Who’s that? Nate: TROY AIKMAN! AIKMAN’S OUT OF HIS SEAT! Robbie: Whaaaaaat? Aikman, who has somehow eluded security, is standing in the aisle, holding a... football? Aikman drops back three steps, and throws the football. It ascends... ascends... ascends... AND STRIKES IVAN IN HIS AMPLE GUT! Stanislav lets out a surprised “Oof!” and drops Cisco, who grabs onto the top tier with all 11 fingers to prevent a nasty tumble. The football lands on the edge of the top tier, as Ivan staggers away. Nate: FOOTBALL TO THE GUT! TROY AIKMAN JUST SAVED CISCO FROM ELIMINATION OR WORSE BY IVAN STANISLAV!!!! Robbie: Just when you think you’ve seen everything... Wayne sits back, watching, as Cisco goes for the football, and cracks it open. Nate: THAT’S NO ORDINARY FOOTBALL! Cisco reaches inside the faux pigskin, to extricate— Six... Finger... Crisco!!!! Holding up a sizable vat of the greasy gunk, Cisco removes the plastic lid. He takes a handful and, as Ivan turns, throws it into Stanislav’s face! Ivan howls in surprise and pain! Ivan: DYAAAAA! IT BURNS! IT BURNS! Nate: WE’RE IN THE FINAL SECONDS! Jeffrey Wayne catches Ivan with a dropkick, then he and Cisco execute a double dropkick—AND IVAN GOES TUMBLING DOWN!!!!! Crowd: [counting] 10! ... 9! Nate: OH MY GOD! OH MY GOD! Crowd: 8! ... 7! Robbie: CRISCO FU!!!! Duke: IVAN’S BECOME A BLOODY GREASEBALL! Paulo: There’s no need for ethnic slurs. Crowd: 6! ... 5! Cisco starts to stand, gasping for breath, when Wayne attacks him! He clocks Cisco with a Roaring Elbow, and shoves him down the incline! Crowd: 4! ... 3! ... Wayne then kicks the vat of Six Finger Crisco off as well, and it lands near the dazed Freak—but on its side! Slowly but surely, the viscous greasy substance starts to spread down the lower incline. Robbie: JEFFREY WAYNE IS THE LAST MAN STANDING!!!! Crowd: 2! ... 1!... A HAND COMES UP ONTO THE TOP INCLINE! DING DING DING DING! Nate: WAIT A MINUTE! Wayne whirls to see a hand belonging to an unseen individual trying to assert its grip. The hand leads to an arm—and the face of... Nate: Oh, fuck me... Robbie: MATT REGISTER! IT’S MATT REGISTER! The crowd is murmuring in excitement now, as two individuals remain on top. Duke: We’re at the end of the time limit, and we have two men left on top! Matt Register AND Jeffrey Wayne! Tag team partners!!! Register and Wayne are eyeing each other cautiously. Cisco is on the middle tier, trying to recover, while Ivan, Tomas, Miguel, Ming, Inphino, Stewart and Hiro are on the bottom. Ming, of course, still is handcuffed, while Asa and DarkStar are only now pulling their beaten bodies out of the bottom cage. Earl: Ladies and gentlemen, since there is no winner at the end of the 60-minute time limit, we will now enter a special OVERTIME period. The first individual to reach the top of the summit by him or herself will be the NEW Old School Champion! With those words, Earl sparks another tumult of activity. Register and Wayne approach one another—and begin to brawl! Meanwhile, Ivan starts trying to climb—but promptly slides back down to the bottom tier! Robbie: What the hell?? Ivan starts to climb again, and again his feet give way and he slides back to the bottom tier. A close-up shows the Russian Bear’s face reddening in frustration—or perhaps in reaction to the Crisco—and also shows shiny buildup on his boots and legs. Nate: It’s the Crisco! The Crisco’s all over Ivan! He’s too greasy to climb!!! Tomas, Miguel and Inphino all are climbing up—when suddenly they start sliding down as well! The greasy Crisco has oozed halfway down that first incline! Robbie: HEY!!! Duke: Now the incline’s all greased up too!!! Nate: Six Finger Crisco has made it near IMPOSSIBLE to reach that middle tier!!! Robbie: You mean Cisco. Nate: No, I mean Crisco. Duke: Actually, shouldn’t we be blaming Troy Aikman? Paulo: GAHHHHHHH!!!!! Up top, Wayne has taken Register to the mat. Pinning his partner against the canvas stomach-down, Wayne starts delivering repeated elbow smashes to his back and neck! The Legend then rolls over, and starts to go for the Crippler Crossface. Nate: Legend Lock on the way! But Register squirrels free, grabs Wayne’s arm and brings him down into a Fujiwara armbar. Wayne starts rolling onto his side before Register gets it hooked, but the Smart Ass extends a leg, kicking Wayne in the ribs and then shoving him away. The Smart Ass stands, but Wayne charges with a roaring elbow! Register ducks it, hooks Wayne in a half nelson and hits a modifed Rock Bottom. Robbie: SMART REMARK!!! SMART REMARK!!! Cisco is up now, and starting to scale the tier, as Register rolls Wayne over and covers. One... Two... Wayne kicks out! Register immediately yanks Wayne up and applies a three-quarter nelson. Nate: Register is setting for the Little Dose of Sarcasm! But suddenly, Wayne breaks Register’s grip! He pulls the Smart Ass close, and connects on an Exploder Suplex! Register lands hard on his hip, with his legs dangling over the edge! Cisco, who is badly favoring his back, is almost up the incline behind the combat as Register starts to stand—but takes a baseball slide dropkick from Wayne! Register gets sent rolling down the incline!!! Robbie: THAT’S IT!!!! Wayne stands, and raises his arms in triumph—but does not see the hand gripping for position behind him. The six-fingered hand. The crowd roars as Six Finger Cisco pulls himself back up onto the tier behind an unaware Jeffrey Wayne. Nate: IT’S CISCO! THE NATIVE SON OF THE LONE STAR STATE IS STILL IN THIS MATCH!!! Robbie: [groans] Back below, the now greasy bottom tier competitors have given up trying to climb. Hiro has already dropped down inside the bottom cage, and is using a ladder to quickly climb into the middle cage. Inphino and Tomas are just behind. Duke: It’s a race! Here come some more guys through the cage itself!!! Ivan, meanwhile, is so coated with Crisco at this point that he can barely stand. On the top, Wayne has turned—into the grip of Cisco’s Six Finger Discount! Wayne starts to thrash as Cisco cinches it, and the Legend immediately starts to fade! Nate: YES! SIX FINGER DISCOUNT! IF WAYNE TAPS, CISCO WINS THE TITLE! Below, Hiro is trying to pull the ladder into the middle cage after him, but Inphino and Tomas grab it and yank down, driving Hiro’s head into the cage itself. Inphino then starts to climb, with Tomas just behind. Cisco’s eyes are dancing with the thought of OSW gold, as Wayne slumps forward against him—and Matt Register reappears on the top tier! He charges forward, shoving Cisco in the back! Cisco and Wayne both go spilling over the side... ... Leaving Matt Register the lone individual atop the mountain. Nate: SHIT! Robbie: YEEEES! Paulo: IT’S OVER! Duke: NEW CHAMP! DING DING DING! The fans start to boo—and boo LOUDLY—as Matt Register drops to his hands and knees. “Cowboys From Hell” plays as Speedy Riggs raises Register’s hand. Earl: Here is your winner, and the NEEEEEEW OLD SCHOOL CHAMPION, MAAAAAATT REEEGISTEEEERRRRRRR!!!! Register raises his arms in triumph, as the bustle of activity inside the cages promptly stops. Nate: WE’VE SEEN CAREERS THREATENED! WE’VE SEEN BLOOD! WE’VE SEEN CHAOS! WE’VE SEEN OSF SQUARE OFF AGAINST OSW! BUT AT THE END, IT’S BEEN MATT REGISTER THAT EMERGES AT THE TOP OF THE MOUNTAIN—AS THE NEW OLD SCHOOL INTERDIVISIONAL CHAMPION! Register is walking back and forth on the top of the mountain, as the Old School Title slowly lowers from the rafters. The Smart Ass unclips the championship title and clasps it around his waist as Jeffrey Wayne and Cisco start to stand on the middle tier. Those on the bottom tier look up as well, knowing how tantalizingly close they came to the championship. Nate: Fans, we are desperately out of time! For Robbie Decker, Paulo DaVatta and Duke Williams, this is Nate Dumas—GOOD NIGHT! Paulo: Hey, I’m sposed to sign of— Paulo is cut off, as the OSE copyright logo appears and the picture fades to black. The Winner of the Match by 'Survival': 'Matt Register'
